398304|||Preface\nBlood of the sky, memories of humans\nThe sky said while establishing the world.\n“Light gives birth to life, and darkness tests it.”\nThus, the sky created humans to convey its will.\nHowever, humans soon forgot the will of the sky and considered themselves gods.\n❖\n20,000 years ago,\nthere was a land where the gate of the sky first opened.\nIt is now called Baikal,\nbut its old name was Hwan-guk (桓國) — a sanctuary called 'the origin of the sky'.\nDescendants of the sky descended to that land.\nThey called themselves Cheonbu (天符).\nStanding between the sky and humans,\nthey were beings who conveyed light, language, iron, fire, and the path of the stars.\nThe sky gave them three sacred objects.\n― Language of the sky, Cheonbugyeong (天符經)\n― Seal of order, Cheonbuin (天符印)\n― Sword of judgment, Cheonbu-geom (天符劍)\nThrough those sacred objects, the will of the sky was planted on the ground,\nand Hwan-guk grew beyond a thousand years\ninto a huge empire where starlight, wind, water, and fire were in harmony.\n❖\nAt the center of Hwan-guk was the city that touched the sky, **Cheonseong (天城)**.\nAt its central altar, **Cheonjedan (天祭壇)**,\nthe Cheonbuju governed the world while communicating with the sky.\nThe descendants of the sky established twelve vassal states,\ntheir language was the resonance of the sky,\nand their law was the cycle of the stars.\nHowever, even they who inherited the blood of the sky were not perfect.\nAs the empire expanded and time passed,\nsome of them began to pursue human desires rather than the sky.\nThat day, the gate of the sky closed,\nand the world was submerged in darkness.\n❖\nDescendants of the sky scattered.\nThose who headed east established Baedal-guk,\nand their descendants opened Dangun Joseon\nto continue the will of the sky.\nHowever, the world was already\nmore dyed by human hands.\nThe language of Cheonbugyeong was forgotten,\nthe seal of Cheonbuin was divided,\nand Cheonbu-geom was shattered and disappeared with the collapse of the 4 Shinbu.\n❖\nAnd now, thousands of years later —\nthe blood of the sky still sleeps in human blood vessels.\nOn the day that blood awakens,\nthe sky will shine on the world again.\nForgotten seals will regain their light one by one,\nand those who continue the will of the sky will rise.\nThat day, the balance of light and darkness will break,\nand the empire of the sky — Hwan-guk (桓國) will reappear.\n“The sky did not abandon humans.\nOnly humans forgot the sky.”\n❖\nThis story is\nthe epic of a hero of the Republic of Korea who tries to reconnect the forgotten will of the sky\nand revive the great empire.\nBefore the sky comes\nwe call that era 'barbarism'.\nHowever, they did not call themselves that.\nThey had laws, promises sworn in blood,\nand gods who governed the earth, wind, fire, and ice.\nOnly, they had no characters to record it, and no language to convey it to the world.\nAt the center of that era was a huge lake we now call 'Baikal'.\nThe lake was a god, a boundary, and a battlefield of blood to them.\nAnd centered on that huge mirror of water,\nfour types of humans from east, west, south, and north lived while keeping each other in check.\n________________________________________\n❖ Verse 1--Water of the East\nTo the east of Baikal, there was a tribe of blue totems that lived while moving along the river.\nThere were wave-pattern tattoos on their faces, and warriors wore armor imitating scales.\nThey governed meat, crossed the battlefield on rafts,\nand when fighting, they flowed like water, and sometimes covered like a flood.\nLater generations call them the 'origin of the Blue Dragon'.\n________________________________________\n❖ Verse 2--Bones of the West\nWarriors of the western mountain range made armor with bones and fur of white beasts.\nThe masks they wore were more like\nthe roar of a white beast than human.\nTheir battle was simple.\nThe strongest became king, and the weakest became a beast.\nLater, they are recorded under the name 'ancestor of the White Tiger'.\n________________________________________\n❖ Verse 3--Fire of the South\nThe southern plain was a land of hunting and sacrifice.\nThey called fire the 'mouth of the sky',\nand soaked the altar with the blood of enemies killed in war.\nRed flags were their faith and declaration of war.\nTheir children were placed in front of fire when born to be chosen,\nand only those who endured that choice became adults.\nLater, their symbol becomes the red bird, Zhuque.\n________________________________________\n❖ Verse 4--Ice of the North\nIn the frozen earth and swamps north of Baikal\nthere was a tribe that handled night and poison.\nThey trusted poison more than blades in hunting,\nand dried the bones of the dead to carry like amulets.\nTheir language was short and low,\nand they considered darkness more sacred than fire.\nLater, these are recorded as the 'root of Xuanwu'.\n________________________________________\nThose four tribes worshipped different gods,\ndid not understand each other, and had no reason to coexist.\nThe east plundered on the water,\nthe west roared under the mountain range,\nthe south conquered with fire,\nand the north took revenge in the darkness.\nTo them, war was not an 'incident' but a 'season',\nand blood was not a 'sin' but a 'cycle'.\nHowever, even in their chaos\nit was not that there were no seeds of order.\nThey already distinguished priests, warriors, elders, and children,\nand divided those chosen by god and those not chosen.\nIt's just that that order did not become characters,\nand did not become a law book.\n________________________________________\nAnd one day,\ntheir sky splits.\nNot their god,\nbut 'another sky' descends.\nAfter that day, war did not end,\nbut the reason for war changed.\nThe king of barbarism disappears,\nand the master of the sky takes that place.\nThat is the beginning of the era of Cheonbu.\nChapter 1 — Woman of Fire,\nSea of Wind\nSection 1 — Shrine Maiden on the Sand\n________________________________________\nDawn in the desert was always red like blood.\nAlthough the sun had not yet risen, light was already flickering on the sand.\nPeople called it the 'breath of god', but the shrine maiden Ara knew.\nThat fire was not made by the sky, but by humans out of fear.\nAra stood in front of the altar and whispered while cutting the sand wind with her fingertips.\n“Even today, the sky is silent… Only fire is answering.”\nWomen lined up behind her.\nThey were all warriors and priests with eyes like flames.\nHowever, one identical question remained somewhere in their hearts.\n“Is the sky really watching us?”\nBelow the altar, men and children were praying to the fire.\nThat prayer was an expression of fear, not 'salvation'.\nWhen there was a drought, they blamed the fire, and when a child died, they blamed the god.\nAnd always, that sin returned to the shrine maidens.\nEven today, someone wailed in front of the altar.\n“Shrine maiden! Why doesn't rain come!”\n“Why does the fire only swallow sacrifices and not save us!”\nAra quietly closed her eyes.\nShe knew the answer.\nIt is not that the sky is silent,\nbut that humans have lost the words of the sky.\nAt that moment, at the end of the distant sky\na strange light was moving very slowly.\nNot fire, not the sun —\na light of a color never seen in the desert.\nAra opened her eyes.\nAnd said in a very small voice.\n“The sky… is moving.”\nHowever, not yet, no one knew that it was\nthe first sign announcing the arrival of 'Cheonbu (天符)'.\nSection 2 — Son of the Sea, Cheongha\nAround the time fire was replacing god in the desert,\na completely different fear was dominating people in the sea at the end of the east.\nSea was god.\nHowever, that god never smiled at humans.\nIt swallowed ships, broke villages, and took children.\nAs the wind grew stronger, people believed it was the god's anger,\nand when the wind stopped, they considered it a temporarily permitted forgiveness.\nCheongha (靑河), a boy of the Ruan tribe (流安族), was the first person to reject that belief.\nOn the day he turned fifteen,\nthe tribe was quietly buzzing.\n“That child is throwing his body into the sea.”\n“He's out of his mind. The sea is not the land of the living.”\n“The sea is a graveyard of the dead. He won't be able to return.”\nCheongha said nothing.\nIt was not that he had abandoned fear.\nHe just refused to let fear become an object of faith.\nAt dawn when the storm was raging,\nCheongha walked to the sea without anyone.\nAdults covered their eyes,\nand children held their breath.\nWaves swallowed him.\nAnd on the third day,\nthe sea spat him out again.\nA blue stone was held in his hand.\nThe wind stopped, and the waves split.\nAn elder of the Ruan tribe muttered with a face terrified.\n“The sea… recognized that child.”\nHowever, Cheongha refused to become a god.\nHe placed the stone on the altar and said this.\n“I am not a god.”\n“I am just someone who made a promise with the sea.”\nThat night, starlight was lined up on the sea.\nAnd without anyone knowing,\nthe seed of the eastern Blue Dragon was planted for the first time.\nThat light, in the distant future,\nbecomes the first vow of the **Blue Dragon Shinbu (靑龍部)**, one of the 4 Shinbu.\nSection 3 — Man of the Snowy Mountain, Hojin\nIn the desert, fire was god,\nin the sea, waves were god.\nHowever, there was no god in the western snowy mountain.\nOnly one law existed there.\n“The one who survives is the truth.”\nHumans of the snowy mountain believed in hunting instead of faith.\nPower to defeat beasts,\nenergy that does not stop even when the blade freezes,\nthat was the only proof of life.\nAmong them, the strongest man,\nhis name was Hojin (虎眞).\nWhen he first faced a huge white tiger,\nno one allowed footsteps on that mountain range.\nWithout screams, prayers, or hatred —\ntwo lives were catching their breath toward each other.\nIn the snowstorm\nthe moment the sword and claws collided,\nthe world stopped for a moment.\nThree days later,\na line of blood was flowing long on the snow.\nAnd on that blood,\nHojin was standing holding the tiger's heart.\nHe put the heart to his mouth and said.\n“This blood is my god.\nAll living oaths are made on blood.”\nAt that moment, the wind of the snowy mountain cried.\nAnd the white snow sank as if bowing.\nThat day, people realized for the first time.\n“God is not above the sky,\nbut dwells in the human heart” fact.\nAfter that, men of the snowy mountain\ncalled themselves **White Tiger (白虎)**.\nTheir warriors did not fight to kill enemies,\nbut fought for the 'will' that does not shake before death.\nThat oath, in the future,\none of the 4 Shinbu guarding the west,\nbecomes the first root of **Baekho-bu (白虎部)**.\nSection 4 — Practitioner of Stone, Muga\nIn the desert, there was fire,\nin the sea, there was a wave,\nin the snowy mountain, there was blood.\nHowever, there was only silence in the northern land.\nDeep in the darkness north of Baikal,\na land where black soil and rocks continued endlessly.\nPeople there were those who built stones instead of words.\nThey did not reveal themselves,\ndid not fight,\nand did not pray to god.\nInstead, they built stones.\nEven if the wind blows, snow falls, and time passes\nstone towers that do not collapse.\nA man born at the center of that stone tower,\nhis name was Muga (武迦).\nHe had no words since childhood.\nHe did not hunt, and did not perform sacrifices.\nThe only thing he did —\n“Raising stones, and raising stones again.”\nPeople initially said he was foolish,\nbut one day they realized that the stone tower exactly matches the constellation of the sky.\nOnly then did the elder whisper, trembling with fear.\n“That child did not hear human words.”\n“He heard the 'silence' of the sky.”\nMuga never explained god.\nInstead, he said this.\n“If the sky does not call us\nwe must approach the sky.”\nIn that silence\nhe read the vibration of the earth, calculated the movement of the stars,\nand understood the cycle of life and death.\nHe did not fear fire,\nwas not shaken by waves,\nand did not cling to blood.\nHe replaced god with soil, stone, and silence.\nHis practice, in the future,\nbecomes the prototype of **Hyeonmu-bu (玄武部)** guarding the north.\nWhen they fight, there is no sound or shout.\nHowever, once they move,\n“The earth splits, and even time stops”\nit was conveyed.\nSection 5 — Seeds of the 4 Shinbu\nIn the desert, fire was preparing for rebellion,\nin the sea, a boy was setting a promise while rejecting god,\nin the snowy mountain, an oath of blood was born,\nin the northern land, stone was talking with stars.\nThey did not even know of each other's existence.\nNeither wind nor starlight conveyed it.\nHowever, the sky was already inhaling toward the four directions.\nFire, water, blood, stone —\nthe four paths were slowly, but fatefully,\ngathering toward one center.\nThe question they built while rejecting god,\nthe oath they engraved with blood,\nthe silence they left with stone.\nThe answer to all of that was not yet on the ground.\nHowever, soon, the sky will descend\nand name that question.\nAnd that name is —\nCheonbu (天符).\nChapter 2 — Crack in the Sky, First City Cheonseong\n________________________________________\nSection 1 — Night the Sky Split\nThat night, the sky was different from usual.\nStars did not flow but stopped.\nAs if someone caught them\nand temporarily stopped the time of the sky.\nHojin of the western snowy mountain stopped breathing in the snow,\nAra of the southern desert hardened while looking at the flames,\nCheongha of the eastern sea lost his breath in the waves,\nand Muga of the north stopped his hand raising on the stone.\nAt that moment —\nthe sky split very thinly, but clearly.\nLight did not pour out.\nThere was no thunder, wind, or flame.\nJust a ray of light descended from the sky.\nAnd below it,\nin the middle of the frozen lake\nnine shadows stepped slowly.\nThe one standing at the very front,\na man wearing a white cloak said quietly.\n“I am Cheonurin (天羽麟).\nOne who will write the will of the sky on the ground.”\nEight people stood in a semicircle behind him.\nTaeyun (太部)· Wolyeon (月部)· Haero (海部)· Seongwon (星部)· Pungdo (風部)· Cheolga (鐵部)· Suryeong (水部)· Toan (土部).\nUnder their feet, the pattern of the sky was already shimmering.\nThat day, the sky for the first time\n“chose humans.”\nSection 2 — Pattern of the Sky, Cheonbugyeong\nIn the middle of the frozen lake,\nCheonurin slowly raised his hand.\nThen the light that fell from the sky\nbegan to take the form of stone.\nIt was not metal, nor soil,\nand not fire, nor wind.\nLight itself became a "stone tablet" with a pattern.\nSomeone called it a sacred object, and\nsomeone whispered that it was alive.\nHowever, Cheonurin did not open the stone tablet immediately.\nHe knelt down and said to the eight people standing by his side.\n“This pattern is not to be read, but to be heard.”\nWhen the stone tablet opened\nthere were no letters or symbols.\nInstead, there was a trembling of light.\nHowever, that trembling —\nwas engraved in each soul in different forms.\nThe man of Cheolbu (鐵部) heard it as the rhythm of flames,\nthe woman of Seongbu (星部) saw it as the orbit of stars,\nand the recorder of Wolbu (Wolbu) wrote it as a soundless sentence.\nAnd Cheonurin changed that trembling into words for the first time.\n“一始無始一 (Ilsi-musi-il) —\nAll beginnings are one, and that one does not begin.”\nWhen that phrase ended,\nthe stone tablet emitted light and spread in 8 directions.\nThat light changed into city blueprints, foundation of law, character system, time calculation method, ritual norms, farming map, trade route, weapon production method,\nand permeated the eight people's hearts.\nAt that moment,\nHwan-guk's first law, first tool, first record, first path were born.\nThe sky was no longer far away.\nThe sky became a pattern, and the pattern became human civilization.\nAnd a name was given to that stone tablet for the first time.\nCheonbugyeong (天符經)\n— Symbol of the sky, language of humans.\nSection 3 — Eight Hands, Beginning of Palbu\nAfter the light of Cheonbugyeong disappeared,\nCheonurin slowly stood up and looked at the eight people.\nThey still had no official position, duty, or obligation.\nHowever, the light of different fates was already burning in their eyes.\nCheonurin said.\n“The sky was one,\nbut its will descended divided into eight branches.”\n“Therefore, this empire is not built by one person's power,\nbut by eight hands.”\nAnd he declared **Palbu (八部)** for the first time.\nDepartment Name Role Symbol Department Head\nTaebu (太部) Law and Governance Sun Taeyun\nWolbu (月部) Record and Language Moon Wolyeon\nHaebu (海部) Trade/Diplomacy/Route Wave Haero\nSeongbu (星部) Astronomy/Time/Measurement Star Seongwon\nPungbu (風部) Ritual/Priest/Culture Cloud Pungdo\nCheolbu (鐵部) Fire/Tool/Weapon Flame Cheolga\nSubu (水部) Medicine/Life/Healing Lotus Suryeong\nTobu (土部) Farming/Architecture/Earth Mountain Toan\nAt that moment,\ndifferent lights rose from under each person's feet.\nIn front of Taeyun, light in the shape of a law book,\non Wolyeon's back of the hand, a small star line,\non Haero's shoulder, a wave pattern,\nunder Seongwon's feet, a constellation\nfloated as if engraved.\nIt was not divinity, but responsibility.\nCheonurin said last.\n“The sky gave miracles,\nbut we build civilization ourselves.”\nAnd the vow of Palbu was recorded in history.\n“The sky gives the path,\nand humans cross that path.”\nSince that day,\nHwan-guk's administration, education, trade, law, academics, farming, medicine, military —\nall structures were established in the Palbu system.\nThe sky was no longer a ruler.\nThe sky was order, and humans became those who execute that order.\nSection 4 — Place Where Four Paths Gather\nAround the time the light of Palbu was spreading over the lake,\nthe same trembling was happening in the lands of the four directions.\nAra, the female warrior of the desert,\nfelt a strange airflow tearing the sky\non the altar where the flames did not go out.\nHojin, the man of the snowy mountain,\nheard a sign older than blood in the wind where the beast's cry stopped.\nCheongha, the boy of the sea,\nsaw a current toward a 'center' that was not the sea\nthe moment the waves stopped and the waves pushed back.\nMuga, the practitioner of stone,\nknew that the soil under his palm\nwas ringing from somewhere far away, not the stone tower he built.\nThey, without knowing each other,\nwithout even knowing each other's language,\nwithout even knowing that each other existed —\nbegan to walk toward a single direction.\n**Where the wind of the east moves,\nthe ice of the west splits,\nthe flames of the south shake,\nand the land of the north rings low.**\nThere was nothing there yet,\nbut the place where the 'city' would exist was already decided.\nPure 'center point of the sky' without crying, sacrifice, blood, or stone.\nCheonurin stood there.\nAnd said quietly.\n“This is the place where the sky will descend.”\nWhen those words ended,\nfootstep sounds approached from all directions.\nWoman with desert fire — Ara.\nBoy who brought sea stone — Cheongha.\nMan who harbored an oath of blood — Hojin.\nPractitioner walking the silence of stone — Muga.\nThey did not speak to each other.\nThey did not ask each other.\nHowever, one thing was certain.\n“We were not dragged here.\nThe sky called us out.”\nAt that moment,\n4 directions were established for the first time.\nEast — Blue Dragon\nWest — White Tiger\nSouth — Zhuque\nNorth — Xuanwu\nThe sky opened four paths,\nand in the place where those paths gathered\nsoon the name “Cheonseong (天城)” will be built.\nSection 5 — City Looking Up at the Sky, Cheonseong (天城)\nCheonurin quietly placed his palm\non the center where four people gathered.\nThen the frozen ground\nbegan to beat minutely like a heart that had been asleep for a long time.\nThe trembling started from the ground,\nshook the wind,\nlowered the fire,\ncalmed the water,\nand woke the vibration of the stone.\nAnd a ray of light descended from the sky\nand drew a straight line on the ground.\nThat straight line soon became the **city's first road (大道)**,\nand at the end, the place where the altar would be placed was marked.\nCheonurin said.\n“This castle will be the place where the sky descended,\nand the place that remembers the sky.”\nHe continued and engraved a pattern on the ground.\n“The sky is above,\nand we achieve that will with our hands.”\nWhen he took his hand off,\nlight permeated into the stone and **'Gidan (基壇)'** was formed.\nThat Gidan, in the future,\nthe altar communicating with the sky,\nbecomes **Cheonjedan (天祭壇)**.\nAnd Cheonurin declared to everyone surrounding that place.\n“Today, I call this place **Cheonseong (天城)**.\nIt is a castle where the sky stays,\nand a human castle that 'looks up' at the sky.”\nAra, the woman of the desert, took out fire,\nCheongha, the boy of the sea, put down the stone,\nHojin, the man of the snowy mountain, stuck his sword into the ground,\nand Muga, the practitioner of stone, quietly put his forehead on the soil.\nAt that moment —\ndifferent lights from under the four people's feet\nconnected into one line.\nThat place became the first capital of “Hwan-guk (桓國)”.\nAnd that city\ngained an immortal name that would remain even if the world collapsed.\nCheonseong (天城).\n— The place where the sky descended,\nand the first city humans built toward the sky.\nSection 6 — Cheonbugyeong Stuck in the Ground\nCheonurin lifted the light stone tablet — Cheonbugyeong again\nunder the altar Gidan.\nIt was no longer an object of the sky,\nbecause it became the language and law to build this place.\nCheonurin said.\n“Miracles are of the sky,\norder is of humans.”\nHe laid Cheonbugyeong under the altar Gidan\nand placed his palm on it.\nThen the pattern of light\nspread from the stone tablet to the ground,\nfrom the ground to the four directions,\nfrom the four directions to the entire continent.\nThat pattern did not speak,\ndid not sing,\nand did not command.\nHowever, all life understood that it was the will of the sky.\nAnd Cheonurin\ndeclared the first law to remain in history.\n“The law of Cheonbu is not built with blood.\nBlood only proves the promise.”\nThat day, Hwan-guk's **first vow (誓)** was engraved on the altar.\nThe sky descends the symbol (符)\nThe ground raises the castle (城)\nHumans place order between them\nAt that moment,\nthe fire of the desert did not burn the altar,\nthe water of the sea did not swallow the stone,\nthe blood of the snowy mountain did not permeate the soil,\nthe stone of the north accepted the light in silence.\n4 directions for the first time\nbecame one not by violence but by consent.\nBased on that day,\nthe era of land ended,\nand **the first civilization under the sky — Hwan-guk (桓國)** began.|||

우리집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집집 I didn't care about what was left to do. I needed a break, and I was sick of childish nicknames like "Magical Girl." As soon as the war against the monsters ended, I handed in my resignation like I was running away and rented a small studio apartment.
But the last Cthulhu I spared during the war has no intention of leaving my house.
"The course you have registered for is 'Basics of Swordsmanship'."
For your information, I have no arms and no legs.
...Excuse me? I'm... a mage?
A world destroyed before my eyes, and an unbelievable opportunity given by the one who destroyed it.
I won't let it slip away.
New users keep coming, but it's a dead game regardless.
If this isn't a dead game when I'm suffering this much, then what is?
I turned 15 and entered the academy, and I just wanted to live an ordinary life.
I had my talent tested.
My talent was a Summoner.
I wanted a more close-combat job, but...
Yeah. Being a Summoner is no problem, it's fine.
Summoners are cool.
Controlling cool and pretty summons to fight is cool enough.
If I'm lucky, I can even make contracts with divine beasts or beings from the demon world.
Yeah, that would be nice.
But for some reason, my summons are...
They are all characters I've seen somewhere before.

Part 2. Growth in a new story

The status window opens, too.
But the jobs I can choose from are strange.
The hero suddenly asked the guy who appeared out of nowhere to be his mentor. Oh my god! I can't let the hero be taken by such a suspicious person!!
"Would you happen to be my mentor?"
"No."
...Huh? He rejected the offer. Wait a minute. Who is that person...?
"No. To me, the only hero is the previous hero."
"Mr. Ad!?"
He was one of the heroes from the previous hero's party.

I tried to live well and eat well with the family I finally got, but why are the female leads obsessed with me..?
"Damn it... If I knew I was going to die like this, I would've lost my virginity before joining..."
As I was engulfed in the light of the 'clock' along with my complaints... for some reason, I was back at my first day of work!

But it turned out to be a gathering place for monsters······.
A berserker who threw away his sword appeared there.
And he's... obsessed with pure love...
One day, she opened her eyes in a strange alleyway, and it was 1925 Gyeongseong.
An era where surveillance and violence by the Japanese Empire were daily life.
In front of her, as she wandered looking for a way back, appeared Yunjae, a young man of Joseon.
"Here, just surviving is an act of courage."
The timid girl gradually becomes stronger,
And a young man throws himself into the independence of his motherland.
Two people watching each other's paths from their respective eras.
A modern actress and a Gyeongseong independence activist.
Their story, transcending time, begins.
...Until that Demon King died and dumped his entire throne onto me.

The knife fell from the man's hand and made a clanging sound on the floor. However, he didn't give up easily and rushed forward, pushing his fist with resentment. I lightly twisted my body and avoided his attack.
"This X-like bastard.."
As he swung his fist and charged again, I swung the fan strongly at his chest.
(Thud!)
It was a strong impact that shook the wall. The big man who fell to the floor screamed in pain and writhed. I slowly approached, looked down at him, and said quietly.
"Regret what you did for the rest of your life"
(Beep beep)
Police arrive at the right timing.
"Catch these bastards all!"
A police officer said. The big men in the office are led by the police one by one and get into the police car.
I stand with the middle-aged man who requested me and watch the scene.
"Customer, are you a bit relieved now?"
I said to the middle-aged man with a smiling face.
Then the middle-aged man answered with tears.
"Yes. Thank you. How to repay this grace.. I will remember this grace even after I go up to heaven. And please deliver the letter to my wife and son well."
[Munchkin Office]
"Customer, it's time for you to leave now."
For a soul to go up to heaven, it is possible only if it dies one more time. The most painless way to die is to enter a chicken's body and be killed.
The middle-aged man's soul entered the chicken's body.
(Pak~!)
I broke the chicken's neck. Thus, the middle-aged man's soul headed to heaven.
"Goodbye. Sir, you had a hard time while you were alive."
[City Hall Civil Service Office]
"Yes. Please come, number 59."
A woman hands over a document with a tearful voice.
"I'm here to report a death."
The woman has a face full of worry, as if her future is bleak.
"You came for a death report. There is something the police station asked to deliver regarding this deceased person. Just a moment."
I handed over a letter and a bundle of money.
"They said it was a letter and money left by your husband. They said it was found in the office where he worked, I don't know why they gave it to me at city hall to deliver, but I will deliver it anyway. I hope you live well in the future."
The woman seemed dumbfounded by the unexpected letter and money. Still, although her heart is heavy due to her husband's death, it seemed like she received a small comfort.
'It might be hard to endure right now, but I hope you live well...'
I watched until the woman's back disappeared and could not be seen. The woman's back slowly disappeared like a cloud under the red sunset.

They are mainly planets in the Orion, Sirius, and Pleiades star clusters.
The war of gods in the Indian Hindu scripture 'Bhagavad Gita' describes the war between aliens and Earthlings at that time. The god 'Jehovah' who ruled the Israelite tribes was also just a captain of an alien planet's mothership. They periodically visited Earth to monitor the development process. Aliens are still hiding and living among humanity. Alien bases are hidden in the hollow interior of the Earth, deep sea trenches, and planets in the solar system. They constantly monitor humanity to prevent them from leaving Earth and incite wars to destroy each other's civilizations. Aliens can manipulate the thoughts of Earth's political and religious leaders. So, they destroyed civilizations through war, and new primitive civilizations would start in their place.
They also tried appeasement policies. At one time, they sent their advanced souls to various regions of the world to enlighten humanity. They are none other than prophets like Confucius, Laozi, Buddha, and Jesus.
That series of attempts all failed. Because humanity had ancestors with too strong militant and combative DNA, humanity was not enlightened. Far from being enlightened, their great messages were distorted into religious forms, distorting the truth and putting them in the ranks of gods, committing blasphemy. Jesus was not the first person to come to claim his divinity, but the first person to complete Christ as a great prophet who preached the divinity within all humanity. Muhammad was not sent as a prophet, but as a false prophet sent to incite war and division. All religions made people believe in alien human leaders, not the true God.
Now, the object humanity must overcome is not themselves, but freedom from the various kinds of gods we have been believing in. We are bound by gods. We are constrained by gods. Daily prayers and weekly worship are keeping us from being free.
And humanity must discover the true God. We must set out to find the 'I AM THAT I AM' that created the universe. Let's go inside. The spirit of God resides deep within oneself. That is each person's true self, and God himself. Now, alien humanity must also take off the mask of God themselves and tell the truth to Earth's humanity.
The distortion of truth must stop now. Peace and love will come to humanity's hearts. We will no longer envy, be jealous, or murder. When we discover the God within, we will become one with God. Just as it was in the beginning....
So I just gave up.

When boiling the noodles, boil plenty of water, and add plenty of salt, thinking of seasoning the boiling water.
And add a little olive oil when boiling.
Grease a pan with olive oil, and add garlic and peperoncino.
If you like spicy food, crush the peperoncino and add it.
Add the noodles and stir-fry.
Add about 2-3 tablespoons of basil pesto.
Stir-fry so that it mixes well with the basil pesto.
At this time.. adjust the consistency by adding a little pasta water.
Season with salt and pepper.
Transfer to a bowl and sprinkle with parmesan cheese, and it's done.

[The glolive Chronicles]
[Chapter 1: Dawn (2025)]
January 15, 2025, the beginning of everything. Haneul starts her first broadcast under the name 'glolive'. As a founding member and spiritual pillar, she began to pioneer the barren VTuber market. Her sincere communication becomes the foundation for forming a powerful early fandom later called the 'Knights of the Round Table'.
April 20 of the same year, the second member Roboco joins. Her unique setting as a high-performance cyborg and her clumsiness brought new vitality to glolive.
June 10 of the same year, the third member Park Youngmong debuts. The concept of an elite shrine maiden and her savory dialect, which is the exact opposite, became an opportunity to further expand glolive's fan base.
September 5 and November 28 of the same year, two talented people from music labels, Yoon Aji and Yoo Sebyeol, join one after another, and glolive finally completes its '0th generation' lineup and enters a full-scale growth trajectory. These five pioneers will later be called the 'Legendary 0th Generation'.
[Chapter 2: New Wave (2026 ~ 2028)]
2026, 1st generation Elia, Yoo Sungah, Ha Yoon, Han Yeoreum, Yoo Seha, and original member Cha-ah debut, and glolive enters a full-scale expansion period. With the joining of those with different charms and talents, glolive's world becomes even richer.
2027, 2nd generation Aya, Yoo Choco, Song Aram, Song Arin, and Yoo Jion, who have stronger personalities such as Oni, Devil, and Gamer, appear, and glolive begins to form a strong 'dormitory culture' and 'rival structure' within the fandom.
2028, 3rd generation Kang Tokki, Song Hwayeon, Kang Eunha, Pirate Captain Mira, and Han Nabi, with a 'fantasy' concept, debut. Their appearance becomes a decisive opportunity for glolive's story to expand beyond simple broadcasting into one huge 'fantasy worldview'.
[Chapter 3: Age of Empire (2029 ~ Present)]
From 2029 to 2032, 4th generation, 5th generation, and 6th generation with a 'secret society' concept debut successfully one after another, and glolive KR headquarters reigns as the absolute powerhouse in the VTuber market.
2033, thanks to the popularity of the headquarters, the first special unit **'Gamers' (Harang Mio, Song Mungchi, Kim Oksun)** is successfully launched.
From 2034, overseas branches such as ID, EN, CH, and JP are established one after another, and new VTubers containing the culture and characteristics of each country are born, and 'glolive' develops into a global empire in name and reality.
After 2051, new experimental units centered on music and art, **'DEV_IS' (ReGLOSS, FLOW GLOW)**, debut, and glolive's worldview is expanding endlessly.

P.S. Notes before reading this. (Must read).
This piece is.
With the feeling of 'It was there? ───It wasn't there.'
It's a pseudo-tertiary creation written with the feeling of a parody of a parody because the material of some works whose time has stopped seems a waste, even the remake that returned after being drafted.
Well, those who have been sitting in this field for a long time will know the old work that you can't see now is the main motif, and besides that, it feels like I stole some material from works that clicked, disappeared, or aren't serialized, so it feels like a rip-off, so you might get the feeling of 'Huh? I've seen this work somewhere'.
In that sense, it can be proudly called the author's personal self-satisfaction piece. I think there will be enough misunderstandings or likes/dislikes from the start.
In that sense, I'm writing this long P.S. in the introduction in advance, so please know this and filter it if you want to filter it. (Just in case, I also put tags)
Of course, if you have a similar mind to me, or if you don't care, I would be really grateful if you could run with me slowly like a long marathon!
That's it! Thank you for reading the long notes!

Most of the God of War's wives, those in his second home, and his precious bonds were destroyed in life and soul by this war.
The God of War (the concept of the absolute god of all absolute beings and concept transcendents), who wanted to obtain a happy daily life, could not achieve that dream in the end.
His disciple, party, and younger sister tried to comfort him, but his heart was already shattered. He barely gathered his heart, said goodbye to his younger sister, disciple, and his disciple's party, and left soon after.
His younger sister tried to follow her brother, the God of War, but the God of War told her not to come, saying that everyone would only be unhappy if they were with him.
Eventually, his younger sister and disciple endured their painful hearts with their party and left for another world.
The God of War completely closed his heart, which had always been kind, and spent an eternity without making bonds.
For 18 quintillion 7000 trillion years, he developed his new swordsmanship and martial arts, and threw away all the powers he had devoured and collected until now, except for the power of the God of War, the power of the Shadow Monarch, and the Covenant • Spear of Destiny and Zenith he usurped while becoming a Campione.
Then, as he broke through to a new realm, he accidentally obtained the true power of the God of War.
Having achieved the feat of completing his own power and martial arts, the place he arrived at was Uminari City, where the aura of a God Slayer could be felt.
Will he be able to get the happy daily life he wants??

A story of madmen and people living on top of them, swallowing sand.
The old woman said, dimming the lamp. Mechanical pumps were moving endlessly in her chest, and every time she spoke, there was a sound of air escaping from a hose.
"In the TV, scientists said that if things go on like this, both the North and South Poles will melt due to global warming. People at that time.....
didn't think much of it, and thought only polar bears or penguins would go extinct, but in the end, it was us humans who were in danger of extinction.."
The girl asked.
"Was it because the sea level rose because the North and South Poles melted?"
The old woman said.
"Well.. that's also true, but according to the scientists, an ancient virus that was in the glacier came out as the ice melted.
And many people started dying from that virus, and only a few lucky people like this survived."
And the old woman coughed loudly once and continued.
"But even among those few people, they were classified into people who were slowly dying and people who were fine, and the people here are the people who are slowly dying."
The old woman lifted a small part of the tent and said. The scenery visible in front of the tent was a shantytown attached to a huge wall.
"And a war broke out among the survivors.... To live, I had to kill others..... The few remaining humans also fell into madness. But at some point, the war calmed down."
The girl said.
"Did people reconcile?"
"Rather than reconciliation, it was because there wasn't a single blade of grass anywhere..."
"When we thought that we were the last generation of humans, a great leader gave us hope."
The girl pointed to the huge wall and said.
"Is that great leader the person who built that huge city?"
The old woman said.
"Yes, he is the person who collected the knowledge lost during the war to the end, and based on this, he built a civilization... But resources are limited and humanity is on the verge of extinction, so people who are slowly dying like us could not enter that great city."
The girl said angrily.
"What kind of great leader discriminates against people?"
The old woman smiled and said.
"It's choice and concentration..... And he thought that if war broke out again, it would be annihilation."
"As a result of him thinking about the reason why war occurs, he judged that it was because one group tried to do everything,
so when he built a new civilization, he formed a coalition of cities, not one country,
and made each city develop only one type of science and technology.
He made them dependent on each other to instill the perception that if one side fails, everyone fails together."
"And not all newly born lives die in this place.
Among the children born, if they are not those who are slowly dying, they can be sent to that great city... Child, show me your finger."
The old woman took something out of a drawer and collected blood from the girl's finger.
"Oh... you are a chosen child. An inspector will come from that city soon, so I will send you then."
The girl asked.
"Grandma, why do you save me from bullies and help me, like you did just now?"
The old woman stroked the girl's hair and said.
"Because when you said where you came from earlier, the place you mentioned is the same as grandma's hometown.
Blue pine trees on the hill behind and daisy flowers in the front yard?
That's a scene hard to imagine for a child your age, who did you learn it from? You're good at lying too."
The girl said.
"It's not a lie, it's real!"

One day, Oliver decides to leave for the Star of the Sky with Jay to find his parents.
However, for Oliver and Jay, who have never left the nomad group, the road to find the Star of the Sky was too difficult.
Oliver and Jay meet their helper Mr. K in the first desert city they visit.
With Mr. K's help, after twists and turns, Oliver and Jay arrive at the ground coordinate B2A77Z99 where the Star of the Sky is, and hear about the Star of the Sky project through the Ark's main system.
They also learn that Oliver himself is the private key that can start the project.
Long ago, young Oliver received a precision test on whether he was suitable as a private key.
The system kernel and core of the Star of the Sky Ark were designed as AI with the same structure as the human brain.
Therefore, to become a private key that can give final commands to the Ark, the consciousness system had to be synchronized with the Ark's kernel and core.
The private key means a unique and proprietary identification code, and it is like a key that has the final authority to start the Star of the Sky Ark project.
Young Oliver was judged to be able to become a private key, and the 14-member supreme board of directors unanimously decided on Oliver as the private key.
However, not long after Oliver was decided as the private key, conflict escalated between the pure race and the assimilated race.
Among the children of the pure race, another private key candidate appeared to replace Oliver.
Oliver's parents sent Oliver to a trash nomad village to escape the pure race's murder threats.
Oliver also learns about the reality of the occupying forces and resistance forces through the explanation of the Ark's main system.
The occupying forces and resistance forces are avatar bio-cyborgs whose nervous systems are connected to the two races. And the two races connected to the cyborgs are in a sleeping state in the Ark's glass capsules.
Thus, Oliver learns about his birth secret and the reality of the war through the Ark's main system. He also learns about the only way to stop the war.
The only way to stop the war is to wake up those who are in a sleeping state in the glass capsules.
However, the Ark, which had been in a dormant state for a long time, did not have enough energy to wake up those sleeping in the glass capsules.
Eventually, Oliver gives an energy charging command to the Star of the Sky Ark.
It takes about a year to wake up the chosen ones in the Ark, and during that time, Oliver and Jay leave on an adventure through several cities with Mr. K.
Oliver, Jay, and Mr. K were together out of necessity at first, but friendship and trust build up while relying on each other and traveling together in difficulties.
One day, Oliver learns of the existence of Jason, a pure race who shares the same fate as him.
Jason is of pure blood and has a consciousness structure that can act as a private key like Oliver.
However, the private key must be unique, and two private keys are not allowed.
Jason, who knew this fact well, mobilizes all means and methods to eliminate Oliver.
If Oliver is eliminated, the Ark automatically searches for a new private key to execute the Star of the Sky project. Jason knew well that if that happened, he would be chosen by the Ark as the private key.
Jason created numerous traps to eliminate Oliver.
However, Oliver, Jay, and Mr. K pool their strength and wisely deal with Jason's traps.
One day, near the end of the 1-year Ark charging, Jason finally gathers the pure races scattered around the world to a city named Star of the Sky. And he lures Oliver to this city.
On a day when a magnetic storm was raging, he uses Oliver to plant a virus in the Ark's main system, infecting even the main system's core and kernel, and eventually takes control of the Star of the Sky Ark, intending to redefine the main system's core and kernel system as he wants.
Jason, who has taken control of the Ark, kicks out all the assimilated races from the Ark, puts only the pure races on the Ark, and starts to achieve his plan to depart for another planet one by one.
Everything seemed to be going smoothly according to Jason's intentions.
However, there was one problem.
Jason did not receive the consent of all the board of directors, which is the final step of private key registration.
Jason isolates 7 executives of the assimilated race who did not agree and uses all kinds of persuasion and threats to register himself as the private key, but it was not easy.
Jason's authority over the Ark, which did not complete final registration, was limited.
Meanwhile, the main system's core discovered a problem in the virus spread by Jason through its own self-purification function.
The virus's encryption key deviated from the non-replicable encryption key definition required by the main system.
In other words, Jason's virus could not replace the core's unique encryption key array.
Eventually, without Jason knowing, the main system starts its own core array rollback recovery process.
While proceeding with the core array rollback recovery, the main system discovers records about Oliver, the private key registered in a normal way.
Through the rollback recovery process, information about Oliver, the private key, is recovered.
Finally, Oliver, who learns that the main system has been restored to its original state, finds the Ark again.
Oliver, who finds the Ark again, meets Jason, who has lost his will to fight with all his attempts thwarted.
Oliver learns through Jason the facts he had not known until now.
And he reaches the conclusion that the people who truly need the Star of the Sky Ark are not the assimilated races but the pure races, and makes a major decision.
